# RichText audit: findings register

Appendix to [the audit plan](../RichTextAudit_2026-10-21.md). Generated from the audit workflows of
2026-10-21 to 2026-10-23 at `fa5821c8` (unchanged for these files at `b69f452f`). Each entry is a reviewer's
finding as reported, followed by the independent verifiers' verdicts: bugs, security and performance findings had a
*refute* and a *reproduce* verifier; other kinds had one *feasibility* verifier. **Severity** is the lowest severity
a non-refuting verifier gave. **Status** is *confirmed* when no verifier refuted, *refuted* when every verdict
refuted, *disputed* when verifiers disagreed and *uncertain* when none could decide. **Plan** names the checklist
item that owns the fix. The plan, not this appendix, is the curated list; several entries describe the same defect.
Long reviewer text is shortened where marked.

## Structural command planner (19)

### commands-01

**DeleteTableColumn reads slots[column - 1] out of bounds for a one-cell body row**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:1936` · bug · critical · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: In the DeleteTableColumn branch each row's raw slots come from MarkdownTableCells; when `column < slots.size()` but `column + 1u < slots.size()` is false the code falls into `Add(slots[column - 1u].last, slots[column].last, L"")`. With column == 0 and a row that has exactly one raw slot this indexes slots[SIZE\_MAX]. GFM allows body rows with fewer cells than the header (the parser pads them: DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:885 `range = column < pending.cells.size() ? pending.cells[column] : MarkdownRange{end, end}`), and the only guard is `! insert && columns == 1u` at line 1904, which tests the header column count, not the row's raw slot count.
- **Failure**: Source "\| a \| b \|\\n\| - \| - \|\\n\| x \|\\n", caret in header cell a, DeleteTableColumn. Header and separator rows have 2 slots and are edited correctly; the body row has slots.size() == 1, column == 0, so `slots[column - 1u]` dereferences before the vector buffer: MSVC Debug asserts/aborts the process; Release reads garbage and feeds a wild range to Add(), which either throws out\_of\_range (command fails instead of removing the column) or, if the garbage happens to pass IsBoundary, stages a deletion of unrelated source bytes.
- **Fix**: When slots.size() == 1 (or column == 0 and no previous slot) replace the slot content with an empty cell (`Add(slots[0].first, slots[0].last, L" ")`) or remove it together with the following pipe if one exists; never index column - 1 unless column > 0. Add a test with a short padded body row.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Source "\| a \| b \|\\n\| - \| - \|\\n\| x \|\\n" (or any 2+-column table whose body row has exactly one raw cell, e.g. a pipe-less continuation line "x"), caret in header cell "a" (column 0), DeleteTableColumn via ExecuteMarkdownCommand or BuildMarkdownCommand. Rows 0 and 1 stage correct deletions of " a \|" / " - \|"; for row 2, MarkdownTableCells returns one slot, so RichTextCommands.cpp:1936 reads slots[SIZE\_MAX]. x64/ARM64 Debug: MSVC STL \_STL\_VERIFY "vector subscript out of range" aborts the process; [...]

### commands-02

**Prefix() takes the first text leaf's content line as the item content start, deleting nested heading/quote/list markers**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:302` · bug · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: Prefix() descends `first = nodes[item].firstChild` through any non-text container and then uses `nodes[first].lines.front().source.first` as `prefix.content` whenever that line is the item's opening line. Parser content lines start after every consumed prefix: for an ATX heading the line is `{trimmed.offset, last}` recorded after `trimmed.Take()` per '#' and `trimmed.Trim()` (DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:1211-1227), and Content() records `cursor.offset` after block-quote/list prefixes (line 547). So for `- # Title`, `- > quote` or `- - inner` the item's content start is placed after the nested marker, and every consumer of prefix.content/LabelStart (ShiftItem removeMarker 399, SetList 966/979/981, Task 1060/1079, Join 1121) edits through the nested marker.
- **Failure**: "- # Title" with caret in the title: SetList(None) or OutdentList -> `Add(prefix.first=0, LabelStart=4, L"")` -> "Title" (heading lost). SetList(Ordered) -> `Add(0, 4, L"1. ")` -> "1. Title". ToggleTask -> SetList(Task) -> `Add(4, 4, L"[ ] ")` -> "- # [ ] Title" (a heading with literal checkbox text, although Heading() itself refuses task/heading mixes). "- > quote" + SetList(None) -> "quote": the block quote is silently dropped.
- **Fix**: Only trust the parser line when the first child is a direct Paragraph (the task-label case the comment describes); otherwise fall back to the padding rescan at 309-312. Reject SetList(Task)/None and marker removal when the item's first child is a heading, quote or list (as Heading() already rejects task items).
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: "- # Title" (RichMarkdown/GFM profile), caret inside "Title": SetList(None) or OutdentList (Shift+Tab on a top-level item) -> edits {0,4}->"" -> "Title" (heading silently deleted). SetList(Ordered) -> {0,4}->"1. " -> "1. Title". ToggleTask -> SetList(Task) -> {4,4}->"[ ] " -> "- # [ ] Title" (literal checkbox text inside a heading, which Heading() itself refuses to create). "- > quote" + SetList(None)/OutdentList -> "quote" (block quote silently dropped). [...]

### commands-03

**Header-row deletion drops the header EOL when the promoted row is the last line without a trailing EOL**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:1857` · bug · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: Header promotion builds `value = Slice(promote.content.first, promote.source.last)` and replaces `[row.content.first, row.source.last)` with it, then deletes the promoted row. TableRow source ranges include the line's EOL (`block.source = {…, line.source.last}` with `{cursor.offset, lineEnd_}` in ContinueTable, DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:452/514), so the header's own EOL is replaced by the promoted row's EOL. When the promoted row is the final line of the document it has no EOL, and the header line is joined with the delimiter row.
- **Failure**: Source "\| a \|\\n\| - \|\\n\| x \|" (no trailing newline), caret in header, DeleteTableRow. Edits: [header.content.first, 6) -> "\| x \|" (no EOL), [12,17) -> "". Result "\| x \|\| - \|\\n": one physical line, no longer a table; the delimiter row is now literal text of the promoted header.
- **Fix**: Replace only `[row.content.first, Line(row).bodyEnd)` with the promoted row's body `[promote.content.first, Line(promote).bodyEnd)` and delete `[promote.source.first, promote.source.last)` including its own EOL; or append `Eol(headerLine)` to value when the promoted row lacks one.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Source "\| a \|\\n\| - \|\\n\| x \|" (17 UTF-16 units, no trailing EOL), caret at 2 inside the header cell, MarkdownCommand::DeleteTableRow. Planner computes header row source {0,6} (includes LF at 5), promoted body row source {12,17} (no EOL since it ends the document), value = "\| x \|". Edits: [0,6) -> "\| x \|", [12,17) -> "". ApplyMarkdownCommand commits "\| x \|\| - \|\\n" with selection {2,2}. The header line and the delimiter line are joined into one physical line; [...]

### commands-04

**InsertTableColumn at column 0 breaks rows that have no leading pipe**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:1922` · bug · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: For rows whose first raw slot exists the column insert is `Add(slots[column].first, slots[column].first, r == 1u ? L" --- |" : L" |")`. On a row without a leading pipe (`a | b`, a form the repo's own tests use) slots[0].first is the first content character, so the inserted text becomes ` |a | b`; MarkdownTableCells trims leading spaces and then strips a leading '\|' as the row's opening pipe (DxUi.MarkdownTableCells.h:13-18), so the new cell is swallowed. The separator row receives ` --- |- | -`, which does not start with '\|' and gains a cell, so header and delimiter counts diverge and the whole table stops parsing.
- **Failure**: Source "a \| b\\n- \| -\\nx \| y\\n", caret in cell a, InsertTableColumn -> " \|a \| b\\n --- \|- \| -\\n \|x \| y\\n". Header parses as 2 cells, delimiter as 3: the block is no longer a GFM table and renders as a paragraph. docs/rich-text.md:1061 promises row/column commands preserve edge pipes.
- **Fix**: Detect whether the raw row starts with an opening pipe (`text[range.first] == L'|'` after trimming) and for column 0 on pipe-less rows insert `| |` / `| --- |` (or ` | ` before the content with an explicit opening pipe) so the inserted cell is not consumed as the edge pipe; add a pipe-less fixture.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Source "a \| b\\n- \| -\\nx \| y\\n", caret {0,0} (header cell "a", column 0), MarkdownCommand::InsertTableColumn. Planner emits Add(0,0," \|"), Add(6,6," --- \|"), Add(12,12," \|") giving " \|a \| b\\n --- \|- \| -\\n \|x \| y\\n". On re-parse MarkdownTableCells trims the two leading spaces and consumes the new '\|' as the row's opening pipe, so the header has 2 cells (a, b) while the delimiter has 3 (---, -, -); [...]

### commands-05

**ToggleStyle/SetLink wrap path accepts selections that partially overlap an existing emphasis/strong/strike/link node**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:1365` · bug · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: When no enclosing node of the requested kind exists, Style() only rejects Html/Image overlap, non-Text lexemes straddling the edges, and partial Code overlap (1365-1380). Nothing rejects a selection whose edge lies inside a Strong/Emphasis/Strikethrough/Link node. The split path has exactly that check (1344-1347: any non-Text inline partially overlapping the selection returns), but the wrap path does not. Link() (1579-1584) has the same gap for emphasis nodes. Because matched delimiter runs are removed from Inlines() (DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:505-521) InlineSpelling sees no straddling unit and returns the raw slice including the inner `**`.
- **Failure**: Source "\*\*ab\*\*c" (visible "abc" with ab bold). Select visible "bc" = source {3,7} and Ctrl+B: replacement `Styled("b**c")` = "\*\*b\*\*c\*\*" -> "\*\*a\*\*b\*\*c\*\*", which parses as strong(a) b strong(c): b lost its bold and c gained it. SetLink on the same selection -> "\*\*a[b\*\*c]\(\<u>)": the link label swallows the closing run, the opener stays literal and the rendered text shows "\*\*ab\*\*c".
- **Fix**: Reuse the split-path partial-overlap guard in the wrap path (and in Link) for every non-Text inline, or extend the selection to the enclosing node's body when the selection touches it.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Source "\*\*ab\*\*c" (visible "abc", "ab" bold). Drag-select visible "bc" (source {3,7}) in Rich view and press Ctrl+B. Style() finds no enclosing Strong, passes guards at 1365-1380, InlineSpelling returns the raw slice "b\*\*c", Styled wraps it to "\*\*b\*\*c\*\*", and Add(3,7) produces "\*\*a\*\*b\*\*c\*\*", which parses as strong(a) b strong(c): "b" silently loses bold and "c" gains it, while the plan reports Changed. [...]

### commands-06

**CodeValue silently drops Math and FootnoteReference inlines (and link destinations) when converting a selection to code**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:787` · bug · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: CodeValue walks the inlines overlapping the selection and handles Text/Code (sliced), Html/Image (reject), Soft/HardBreak (reject) and empty-child Link (reject). Every other kind falls through `continue`: Math and FootnoteReference are leaf nodes with no Text children (DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:1234 `Append(Kind::Math, …)`, 714-717 `firstChild = nextSibling = MarkdownNoNode`), so nothing is emitted for them; a Link with children contributes only its label, discarding destination/title. The staged edit replaces the whole selection, so that source is deleted, violating the contract that unsupported/opaque syntax must never be silently dropped.
- **Failure**: RichMarkdown profile, source "see \$E=mc^2\$ here", select all, ToggleStyle(Code) -> output.text is "see " + " here" -> `Add(0, 17, L"`see here`")`: the formula is gone. "note[^1] end" select all -> "`note end`". "[a]\(https://x)" select all -> "`a`" (destination lost without any rejection).
- **Fix**: Return nullopt (Unavailable) for Math, FootnoteReference and Link-with-children overlap, or emit their raw source as literal units; document the choice.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: RichMarkdown profile, source "see \$E=mc^2\$ here" (17 units), selection {0,17}, MarkdownCommand::ToggleStyle with style Code: BuildMarkdownCommand returns an applicable plan staging Add(0, 17, L"`see here`"); ApplyMarkdownCommand yields "`see here`" and the formula source is silently deleted (semantic display loses the U+FFFC math object). Likewise "note[^1] end" {0,12} -> "`note end`" (the footnote reference, defined or not, vanishes). [...]

### commands-07

**OutdentList removes exactly parent.contentIndent columns, so an over-indented child (4-space nesting) stays nested**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:491` · bug · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: Indent(true) computes `delta = -contentIndent` of the parent item and ShiftItem rewrites each line's indentation to `columns + delta`. CommonMark nests any marker whose indentation is >= the parent's content indent, so a child indented by 4 under a `- ` parent (content indent 2) is left with 2 columns and is still a child. The source changes, history gains an entry, but the structure the user asked to change does not; a second Outdent is required. Specs/UI/UI\_ControlsAndLayout.md:1297 says commands "indent/outdent one level".
- **Failure**: Source "- a\\n - b\\n" (the common four-space style), caret in b, OutdentList -> ShiftItem: columns=4, delta=-2, count=2 -> "- a\\n - b\\n" which still parses as b nested under a. Status Changed, structure unchanged.
- **Fix**: For outdent, compute the target indentation as the item's own column relative to the grand-parent content indent (i.e. reduce to `columns - (columns - parentOffset)` so the marker lands at the parent's marker column), or strip all columns at or beyond the parent's content indent.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Source "- a\\n - b\\n" (parent `- a` has contentIndent 2; child marker indented 4 columns), caret inside "b" (e.g. selection {10,10}), OutdentList (Shift+Tab in Rich view). Indent(true): items=[b], parent=a, delta=-2. ShiftItem: OuterOffset(a) gives start=4, columns=4, guard `columns < 2` does not fire, count=2, Add(4,8," ") -> "- a\\n - b\\n". Reparse: 2 >= contentIndent 2 so b is still nested under a. ApplyMarkdownCommand returns Changed and pushes one history entry; [...]

### commands-09

**InsertParagraph on a non-leaf block inside a block quote deletes the quote marker instead of inserting a line**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:609` · bug · major · confirmed (refute: confirmed; reproduce: confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: When LeafAt() finds no text leaf and the caret is inside a BlockQuote, Continue() assumes the line is a quote-only empty line: it scans over spaces and '>' characters, rejects only if the caret precedes that scan end, and then removes the last '>' marker. It never checks that the line is actually empty after the markers. Any non-leaf block inside a quote (thematic break, reference definition, table delimiter row) with the caret after the prefix takes this branch, so Enter strips the quote level and inserts nothing.
- **Failure**: Source "> \*\*\*", caret at end (offset 5), Enter -> at = 2, `first < at` is false, marker = rfind('>', 1) = 0 -> `Add(0, 2, L"")` -> "\*\*\*" with caret at 3: the thematic break leaves the quote and no new line appears. Same for "> [a]: /u" and the delimiter row of a quoted table (which then splits the table).
- **Fix**: Require `at == line.bodyEnd` (and `first == at`) before exiting a quote level; otherwise fall through to the ordinary EOL + prefix insertion, or treat ThematicBreak/ReferenceDefinition/Table delimiter lines as opaque like the other non-leaf kinds.
- **Verifier (refute, confirmed)**: Source "> \*\*\*" (a thematic break inside a block quote), caret at the end or anywhere after "> ", then InsertParagraph (Enter). The plan is Add(0, 2, ""), giving "\*\*\*" with the caret at 3. The break leaves the quote and no new line is inserted. One undo restores the source.
- **Existing tests**: None for this case. Tests/Support/Scope.Tests.RichSemantic.h:265-271 covers InsertParagraph only for list items, empty items and fenced code.

### commands-08

**Unwrapping strong/emphasis/strike escapes every ASCII punctuation character in the body, not only context-sensitive ones**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:1334` · bug · minor · confirmed (refute: confirmed; reproduce: confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: The unwrap branch calls 'InlineSpelling(leaf, \*body, L"!\\"#\$%&'()\*+,-./:;\<=>?@[\\\\]^\_'{\|}~", true, false)', and InlineSpelling pushes an escape for every single-unit literal Text punctuation inside the range (835-845) and stages 'Add(at, at, L"\\\\")' for each (857-859). The comment and docs/rich-text.md:1058 describe escaping "when the changed delimiter context would reinterpret it" (a block marker at line start, a merged delimiter run, a table pipe), but the implementation escapes commas, periods, parentheses, apostrophes, etc. anywhere in the body. Because the resulting '\\,' becomes an EscapedPunctuation lexeme, re-wrapping keeps the backslashes, so one toggle permanently pollutes the source.
- **Failure**: "\*\*Hello, world! (see e.g. foo)\*\*", caret inside, Ctrl+B -> "Hello\\, world\\! \\(see e\\.g\\. foo\\)". Ctrl+B again -> "\*\*Hello\\, world\\! \\(see e\\.g\\. foo\\)\*\*". Rendering is unchanged but the raw Markdown (the document the user owns) now carries 8 spurious escapes that other tools display verbatim.
- **Fix**: Restrict unwrap escaping to (a) the first non-space unit of each physical line when it could start a block construct (#, >, -, +, \*, digit+./), (b) `*`/`_`/`~` units adjacent to the removed delimiters, (c) `|` inside table cells. Keep LiteralMarkdown's full escaping for code removal only, as the docs state.
- **Verifier (refute, confirmed)**: Start with "\*\*Hello, world! (see e.g. foo)\*\*", caret inside, then ToggleStyle Strong (Ctrl+B). Result: "Hello\\, world\\! \\(see e\\.g\\. foo\\)", with 6 escapes added. Select the whole line and press Ctrl+B again. Result: "\*\*Hello\\, world\\! \\(see e\\.g\\. foo\\)\*\*". The escapes persist because they are now 2-unit escaped-punctuation units, which InlineSpelling skips. Rendering is identical, and undo restores the exact original.
- **Existing tests**: None pins the raw output. Tests/Support/Scope.Tests.RichSemantic.h:317 tests "\*\*abc\*\*" -> "abc", which has no punctuation. Tests/Support/Scope.Tests.RichSemanticDocument.h:383-426 checks unwrap of "\*\*# heading\*\*", "\*\*- item\*\*" and "\*\*1. item\*\*" by display equality only.

### commands-10

**SetList paragraph path lacks the nine-digit ordered-marker overflow guard**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:1021` · bug · minor · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: The selected-items path throws when the running number exceeds 1e9 (961-965), but the paragraph-to-list path increments `number` per content line without any bound. CommonMark ordered markers are limited to nine digits, so a ten-digit marker is literal text.
- **Failure**: listStart = 999999999 (accepted at line 926), selection covering two paragraph lines -> "999999999. a\\n1000000000. b": the second line is not a list item; it becomes lazy continuation text of the first item, so the user gets one item whose text contains "1000000000. b".
- **Fix**: Check `number > 999999999u` before emitting each marker in both paths (and throw/reject once, before staging), matching Continue()'s `marker.number == 999999999u` guard.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: MarkdownCommandRequest{command=SetList, listKind=Ordered, listStart=999999999} on source "alpha\\r\\nbeta\\n" with selection {0,12} (the exact fixture at Tests/Support/Scope.Tests.RichSemantic.h:280 with a different start). Line 926 accepts the start. Paragraph path: first line gets "999999999. " (number -> 1000000000), second line gets "1000000000. " with no check, both staged via Add (line 1026). [...]

### commands-11

**IndentList appends trailing spaces to blank lines inside a loose item**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:413` · bug · minor · confirmed (: confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: ShiftItem skips blank lines only when `delta < 0` (the `columns < -delta` branch). For a positive delta every line in the item, including interior empty lines, receives `Add(start, end, std::wstring(columns + delta, L' '))`, so an empty line becomes whitespace-only. ShiftFollowing (448-451) does skip them. docs/rich-text.md:1030 and the spec promise untouched spelling stays exact.
- **Failure**: Source "- a\\n- b\\n\\n c\\n", caret in b, IndentList (previous sibling a, delta 2) -> "- a\\n - b\\n \\n c\\n": the empty line gained two trailing spaces that were never part of the item.
- **Fix**: Hoist `if (end == line.bodyEnd) continue;` above the delta test so blank lines are never rewritten for either direction.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Source "- a\\n- b\\n\\n c\\n" (item b is loose: blank line then continuation paragraph " c"), selection {4,4} inside "- b", MarkdownCommand::IndentList. Indent(false) finds previous sibling a (contentIndent 2) -> ShiftItem(b, +2). Lines visited: "- b" at 4 gets Add(4,4," "); the empty line at 8 (first==bodyEnd==8) gets Add(8,8," ") because the blank-line `continue` at line 415-416 is only reachable when delta \< 0; " c" at 9 gets Add(9,11," "). Applied result: "- a\\n - b\\n \\n c\\n". [...]

### commands-12

**Indent/Outdent/SetList(None) of a nested loose item with an empty interior line is rejected**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:230` · bug · minor · confirmed (refute: confirmed; reproduce: confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: OuterOffset requires `takeSpace(owner.contentIndent)` for every ListItem ancestor on non-opening lines and returns `column == target`, which is false on a zero-length line. ShiftItem (395-396) and ShiftFollowing (436-437) treat that as a hard rejection before their own blank-line skips run, so any item with a ListItem ancestor beyond the shift owner and a truly empty interior line cannot be shifted. The documented rejection class ("missing lazy container prefixes") does not cover blank lines, which need no prefix.
- **Failure**: Source "- a\\n - b\\n - c\\n\\n d\\n", caret in c, OutdentList -> ShiftItem(c, -2, false, parent b) -> OuterOffset(b, blank line): parents = [a], takeSpace(2) on an empty line returns false -> Unavailable. Same for IndentList on c and for SetList(None).
- **Fix**: In ShiftItem/ShiftFollowing, `continue` for lines with `line.first == line.bodyEnd` before calling OuterOffset (or let OuterOffset succeed trivially on empty lines).
- **Verifier (refute, confirmed)**: "- a\\n - b\\n - c\\n\\n d\\n" with the caret in c. OutdentList and SetList(None) both return Unavailable, because OuterOffset fails takeSpace(2) for ancestor a on the zero-length line. For IndentList, use a nested item that has a previous sibling and an empty interior line: "- a\\n - x\\n - b\\n\\n c\\n" with the caret in b is rejected.
- **Existing tests**: None covers a nested item with an empty interior line. Tests/Support/Scope.Tests.RichSemantic.h:270 ("- parent\\n\\n - \\n") places the blank line outside the shifted item, and its outdent owner has no ListItem ancestor.

### commands-13

**Table capacity boundaries signal inconsistently: one-off limits throw while larger ones return Unavailable**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:1777` · bug · minor · refuted (refute: refuted; reproduce: refuted) · plan: not scheduled (refuted)

- **What**: `rows.size() > 257u || columns > 64u` returns a non-applicable plan (1718), but `rows.size() == 257u` in insertRow and `columns == 64u` in InsertTableColumn throw length\_error (1777, 1902). Through the editor, Tab in the last cell of a 257-row table or Enter in a table cell therefore throws out of ExecuteMarkdownCommand; OnKeyDown/OnChar swallow it (`catch (const std::exception&) { return false; }`) and report the key as unhandled, so the host performs focus traversal instead of the documented Unavailable fallback.
- **Failure**: Table with 256 body rows, caret in the last cell, Tab -> NextTableCell -> insertRow throws length\_error -> OnKeyDown returns false -> focus leaves the editor; the public ExecuteMarkdownCommand API, documented to return Changed/Unchanged/Unavailable, throws for this input but returns Unavailable for a 258-row table.
- **Fix**: Pick one policy per contract: treat at-limit as Unavailable in the planner (return without applicable\_) and reserve exceptions for malformed requests; or catch length\_error in ExecuteMarkdownCommand and map it to Unavailable.
- **Verifier (refute, refuted)**: The two outcomes are a deliberate split that the tests pin: admission versus capacity overflow. Admission: docs/rich-text.md:1063 says table commands "admit at most 64 columns, 256 body rows and 4,096 declared cells". rows includes the header, so rows.size()==257 is exactly the documented maximum and is admitted. A 258-row table is not admitted and returns Unavailable at 1718. Capacity overflow: a command that would grow past a limit throws length\_error. [...]
- **Existing tests**: Tests/Support/Scope.Tests.RichSemantic.h:433-445 (Reject\<std::length\_error> for the table-limit promotion) and 453-457 (Reject\<std::length\_error> for the command capacity check).

### commands-14

**Removing an HTML anchor title leaves the attribute's leading whitespace**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:1548` · bug · minor · confirmed (refute: confirmed; reproduce: confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: When request.title is empty and the validated `<a>` tag carries a title attribute, the planner replaces `range(nameFirst, at)` (the attribute name through its closing quote) with an empty string; the space(s) before the attribute stay, so the tag keeps a doubled or trailing space. The docs claim attribute spacing is preserved.
- **Failure**: `<a title="old" href="u">w</a>` + SetLink(destination="n", title="") -> `<a href="n">w</a>`; `<a href="u" title="old">` -> `<a href="n" >`.
- **Fix**: Extend the removed range back over the whitespace preceding nameFirst (one unit at minimum) when deleting the attribute.
- **Verifier (refute, confirmed)**: Cosmetic case (as reported): `<a title="old" href="u">w</a>` + SetLink{destination="n", title=""} -> `<a href="n">w</a>`. Semantic case: `<a href="u"\r\ntitle="old">w</a>` (or with up to 3 spaces before title) + SetLink{destination="n", title=""} -> `<a href="n"\r\n>w</a>`. The second line now starts with '>', which opens a block quote. The paragraph becomes literal `<a href="n"`, a quote containing `w</a>` follows, and the inert link is lost. No validation rejects this.
- **Existing tests**: None for removing a title. Tests/Support/Scope.Tests.RichSemantic.h:329 covers only a non-empty title update on `<A TITLE='old' HREF = "old">`. Tests/Support/Scope.Tests.RichSemanticDocument.h:190 checks parsing of title-before-href, not the command.

### commands-15

**Header promotion caret `content.first + 2` can land inside a surrogate pair and abort the command**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:1882` · bug · minor · confirmed (refute: confirmed; reproduce: confirmed) · plan: W1.1

- **What**: After promoting a body row the caret is fixed at `nodes[row].block.content.first + 2u` with explicitSelection, assuming the promoted row starts with "\| ". If the promoted row is `|😀|x|` the offset splits the surrogate pair; Finish() then throws out\_of\_range ("Invalid structural command result selection"), so the whole command fails from the pure API and is swallowed by the editor's key handler. Every other table branch computes carets from rawCellPosition or inserted text.
- **Failure**: Source "\| a \|\\n\| - \|\\n\|😀\|\\n", caret in header, DeleteTableRow -> candidate text "\|😀\|\\n\| - \|\\n", after\_ = {content.first+2} = between the two UTF-16 units of U+1F600 -> IsEditBoundary false -> throw instead of promoting the row.
- **Fix**: Place the caret at the promoted row's first cell content (`nodes[promote].firstChild` content.first mapped through the edits via MapPosition, i.e. leave explicitSelection false) instead of a fixed +2 offset.
- **Verifier (refute, confirmed)**: Surrogate case: `| a |\n| - |\n|😀|\n`, caret in the header, DeleteTableRow -> candidate `|😀|\n| - |\n`, after\_ = 2 splits U+1F600 -> std::out\_of\_range from BuildMarkdownCommand/ExecuteMarkdownCommand, and the command cannot run. ASCII variant with the same root cause: `| a |\r\n| - |\r\nx\r\n` (GFM continues the table with a pipe-less body row, MarkdownBlocks.h:1087-1095). The candidate `x\r\n| - |\r\n` puts after\_ = 2 between CR and LF, which is also rejected by IsEditJoinAllowed and throws. [...]
- **Existing tests**: Tests/Support/Scope.Tests.RichSemantic.h:353 checks only ASCII `| x | y |` promotion text, not the selection. There is no surrogate or CRLF single-character promotion test.

### commands-16

**Every plan, including per-keystroke deletion, performs several full-document passes and allocations**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:119` · performance · minor · confirmed (refute: confirmed; reproduce: confirmed) · plan: W2.13

- **What**: BuildMarkdownDeletion runs on each Backspace/Delete in semantic Rich view (DxUi.RichTextEditor.cpp:1632/1702). Per call: CheckedSyntax materializes the whole snapshot (`source.Text() != syntax->Source()`) to prove freshness; the constructor rescans every physical line into `lines`; Delete() calls NormalizeSemanticLeaf (text + units vector per character) and NativeHtmlPlan::Inline for every text leaf and HTML block in the document (1983-1999) to collect wrappers, and collects InlineBody for every inline (1980-1982) regardless of where the deletion is; Finish() builds a complete `candidate` copy of the new text only to check two caret boundaries (903-914). NodeAt is a linear scan over all nodes and LeafAt/EmptyStructuralSite/InlineSelection call it 4-5 times each. [...]
- **Failure**: A 60 Ki-unit document with 2,000 paragraphs: each Backspace copies ~120 KB for the freshness compare, normalizes ~2,000 leaves (~1.4 MB of SemanticValue units), re-derives ~5,000 inline wrappers, and copies ~120 KB again for the candidate — roughly four document-sized allocations per keypress before any edit is applied.
- **Fix**: Compare snapshot identity/revision instead of materializing text (MarkdownSyntax could retain the SourceSnapshot it parsed); restrict wrapper collection in Delete() to leaves and inlines intersecting the merged ranges; validate caret boundaries against the local replacement pieces instead of a full candidate; add move constructors to MarkdownCommandPlan/MarkdownCommandEdit (or document why copies are required).
- **Verifier (refute, confirmed)**: Each Rich-view Backspace/Delete in a near-cap document copies the whole source once for the freshness check. It allocates and frees one SemanticValue per text leaf and HTML block, which adds up to about 24 bytes per source unit in total; per-leaf buffers are freed between iterations, so this is total allocation volume, not peak. It re-derives every inline wrapper and builds one more full-size candidate string. That is O(document) work regardless of where the deletion is. [...]
- **Existing tests**: No test or benchmark pins keystroke-deletion planning cost. Scope.Tests.RichSemantic.h:446-448 checks only fragment-count admission (MaximumSpans) before staging.

InlineBody(Code) (1142-1153) re-implements ResolveSemanticValue's fence/pad stripping (DxUi.RichSemanticValues.h:108-136) with a subtly different newline rule. [...]

After: one `TabColumn(column, c)`/`Columns()` helper, one shared `AsciiPunctuation` in a small MarkdownText header used by inlines, LiteralMarkdown and the InlineSpelling callers, one `CodeSpanBody(range)` shared by the planner and the semantic resolver.

InlineBody keeps the raw newlines inside the body, so deleting the whole visible "foo" does not count as covering the wrapper body in Delete(), and the backtick wrapper is not cleaned up the way the single-line `` ` foo ` `` form is. [...]

### commands-19

**Footnote guard and leaf lookups repeat linear NodeAt scans**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextCommands.cpp:2049` · simplification · minor · confirmed (feasibility: confirmed) · plan: W2.13

- **What**: BuildMarkdownCommand's footnote guard iterates all nodes and, for a collapsed selection, calls `builder.NodeAt(FootnoteDefinition, Start)` (itself an O(nodes) scan) once per FootnoteDefinition node, giving O(F·N) work with an identical result each time. LeafAt (167-178) performs four separate NodeAt scans, EmptyStructuralSite five, InlineSelection four, HasOpaqueSelection one more; a command such as Style() ends up scanning the node table a dozen times.
- **Failure**: Before: a document with 500 footnote definitions and 5,000 nodes runs 2.5 M node comparisons before the command switch even for a caret outside any footnote. After: evaluate the collapsed-selection case once before the loop (`if (collapsed) return NodeAt(FootnoteDefinition, Start) != NoNode ? Finish() : …`), and let NodeAt accept a kind set so LeafAt/EmptyStructuralSite are single passes.
- **Fix**: Compute the collapsed-case lookup once outside the loop; give NodeAt a kind-mask parameter (or a precomputed innermost-container index per position) and rewrite LeafAt/EmptyStructuralSite/InlineSelection as single scans.
- **Verifier (feasibility, confirmed)**: Main fix (one or two lines, no behavior change): replace the guard loop with `const bool collapsed = selection.Start() == selection.End(); if (collapsed ? builder.NodeAt(FootnoteDefinition, selection.Start()) != MarkdownNoNode : std::ranges::any_of(builder.nodes, overlapsDefinition)) return builder.Finish();`. That makes the guard O(N). [...]
- **Existing tests**: Tests/Controls/Scope.Tests.RichTextEditor.cpp:910-917 covers the non-collapsed branch of the guard. With selection = "second" inside a footnote definition, it runs ToggleStyle, SetHeading, SetList and InsertTable and expects Unavailable, with source and history unchanged. [...]

## Markdown block parser (15)

### blocks-01

**Reference-definition extraction bypasses the 32,768 node admission limit and can publish an over-capacity tree**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:817` · bug · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: Every other node creation goes through Add(), which throws length\_error when nodes\_.size() >= MarkdownSyntax::MaximumNodeRecords (lines 520-521), and TableRow() pre-admits whole rows (448-449). References() instead does `nodes_.emplace_back(); tails_.push_back(...); previousSibling_.push_back(...); blank_.push_back(false);` (816-820) once per definition with no capacity check. If the definitions-only paragraph is the last leaf, Finish() compacts and the syntax is published with more than MaximumNodeRecords nodes. The inline stage then computes `MarkdownSyntax::MaximumNodeRecords - blocks_.size()` (DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:236 and :296), which underflows to a huge size\_t once blocks\_.size() > 32768, so the combined block/inline budget is disabled for that revision; [...]
- **Failure**: Source of 10,922 lines of `- -\n` (43,688 units; 4 + 3\*(n-1) = 32,767 block nodes after the paragraph Add) followed by 3,641 lines of `[a]:b\n` (21,846 units; total 65,534 \<= Document::MaximumUnits). The paragraph Add succeeds at 32,766 nodes; at EOF Finish()->CloseLeaf()->References() appends 3,641 ReferenceDefinition nodes with no check, compaction publishes 36,407 nodes. MarkdownSyntax.h:237-239 and Specs/UI/UI\_ControlsAndLayout.md:1571-1578 promise length\_error 'without publishing a partial/excessive tree'; [...]
- **Fix**: Route the ReferenceDefinition creation through Add() (or a shared Admit() helper used by Add/TableRow/References), and compute remaining budget with a saturating subtraction (`nodes_.size() >= Max ? 0 : Max - nodes_.size()`) in TableRow and MarkdownInlines::Append/OpaqueHtmlBlock so an overshoot can never wrap. Add a capacity fixture mixing a near-cap list document with a long definitions paragraph.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Source: 10,921 lines of "- -\\n" (43,684 units) followed by 3,641 lines of "[a]:b\\n" (21,846 units), 65,530 units total (\<= Document::MaximumUnits). Block parse: Document(1) + 4 + 3\*10,920 = 32,765 nodes; the first "[a]:b" line passes Add(Kind::Paragraph) at 32,765 \< 32,768 -> 32,766 nodes; the remaining definition lines are Content() continuations. ### blocks-02

**Trailing blank lines of an indented code block lose their caret sites: popped from lines, never recorded in EmptyLines, and the EOF trailing line is suppressed**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:584` · bug · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: Content() records blank lines inside an open IndentedCode leaf as code lines (`if (empty || cursor.Indent() >= 4u) { cursor.Spaces(4u); Content(...) }` at 1069-1073) and extends block.content.last/source.last to lineEnd\_ (552-555). CloseLeaf() then pops every trailing whitespace-only line from node.lines (584-595) but leaves content.last/source.last unchanged, and those lines were never pushed to emptyLines\_ (the empty branch at 1079-1086 is never reached for them). Finish() additionally computes trailingInLeaf \*before\* CloseLeaf() using the still-extended content.last (1297-1301), so the final EOF empty line is not appended either (1335-1336). [...]
- **Failure**: Source ` code\n\n` (10 units): line 2 is pushed as code line {9,10} then popped by Finish's CloseLeaf; trailingInLeaf is true (content.last == 10 == size) so no EOF MarkdownEmptyLine is appended; EmptyLines() is empty, node.lines == [{4,9}]. Source ACP 10 (EOF) has neither a code line nor a caret site, contradicting docs/rich-text.md:945-947 ('Empty, trailing ... source lines have distinct prepared caret sites') and UI\_ControlsAndLayout.md:1217-1222 ('source EOF has its trailing line'). [...]
- **Fix**: When CloseLeaf pops trailing IndentedCode lines, either shrink block.content.last/source.last to the last kept line and push each popped line to emptyLines\_ (owner = parent container, insertion = its bodyEnd) so they become ordinary empty caret sites, or keep them in lines like fenced code does. Compute trailingInLeaf after CloseLeaf. Add `" x\n\n"` and `" x\n\n\npara"` to the SourceCaretSites fixtures.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Source ` code\n\n` (RichMarkdown or CommonMark profile, Rich view): the block recognizer pushes line 2 as code line {9,10} (Content via DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:1069-1074), Finish() computes trailingInLeaf == true from content.last == 10 (1297-1300), CloseLeaf() pops the line (587-594), and no EOF MarkdownEmptyLine is appended (1335). EmptyLines() is empty and node.lines == [{4,9}], while block.source stays {0,10}. The semantic display is "code\\n" (source 4..9); [...]

### blocks-03

**SourceEditor region is attached to open\_.back() without container matching, nesting a top-level block inside a still-open list item or footnote**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:44` · bug · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: Line() tests the editing envelope (947-952) before the container-matching loop (969-1000) and StartSourceRegion() does `leaf_ = Add(Kind::SourceEditor, open_.back())`. ListItem (with children) and FootnoteDefinition containers survive a blank line without being popped (995: blank line only closes an item with no children; footnotes never close on blank), so when the region's first line follows such a blank line the synthetic leaf becomes a child of a container that, under ordinary parsing, the same line would have closed. Finish() then sets every still-open container's source.last to EOF (1302-1308), so the list/footnote range swallows the edited block and everything after it, and the semantic visit indents the SourceEditor by the list gutter.
- **Failure**: Source `- item\n\n```\\ncode\\n```\n`, F2 on the fence (BeginSourceRegion {8,21}). Line 2 (blank) keeps [doc, List, ListItem] open because the item has a paragraph child. Line 3 starts the region: Add(SourceEditor, ListItem) instead of the document; List/ListItem source.last become 21. Ordinary Begin of the same text puts the fence at document level (line 3 fails the item's contentIndent and pops it). The raw editor is therefore presented nested inside the list item with its gutter while editing, and leaves that position on Esc; [...]
- **Fix**: Run the normal container-matching loop for the region's first line, then (without interpreting the line as a block start) close the leaf, pop unmatched containers (`while (open_.size() > matched) PopContainer();`), and only then Add the SourceEditor under the resulting open\_.back(). Add a scenario `- item\n\n```\\ncode\\n```\n` asserting the SourceEditor's parent is node 0 and the list range ends at 8.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Source `- item\n\n```\\ncode\\n```\n`, F2 on the document-level fence (region {8,21}). Blank line 2 leaves [Document, List, ListItem] open (MarkdownBlocks.h 995-996 and 1079-1086 never pop an item with children). Line 3 satisfies the envelope test at 947-948 before any container matching, so StartSourceRegion (41-48) adds the SourceEditor as a child of the ListItem; Finish() 1302-1308 extends List/ListItem to 21. [...]

### blocks-04

**Lazy-continuation decision ignores the 'last matched container' rule used by cmark/commonmark.js for list markers, empty items and type-7 HTML**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:1010` · bug · minor · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: When some container fails to match and the open leaf is a paragraph, `interrupts` applies the paragraph-interruption restrictions (ordered start must be 1, item must be non-empty, HTML type 7 cannot start) unconditionally, with a special case only for a sibling list item (`listSibling`). cmark (`parse_list_marker(..., (*container)->type == CMARK_NODE_PARAGRAPH, ...)`, `cont_type != CMARK_NODE_PARAGRAPH && scan_html_block_start_7`) and commonmark.js (`container.type !== 'paragraph'` in parseListMarker) apply those restrictions only when the last \*matched\* container is the paragraph itself. When the paragraph lives inside an unmatched block quote, both reference implementations open the new block and close the quote; DxUi lazily continues the paragraph. [...]
- **Failure**: `> a\n2. b` -> cmark/commonmark.js: \<blockquote>\<p>a\</p>\</blockquote>\<ol start="2">\<li>b\</li>\</ol>; DxUi: one paragraph `a\n2. b` inside the quote (line 2 reaches 1014 with interrupts == false). Likewise `> a\n-` (empty item) and `> a\n<custom>` (type 7) stay lazy continuations in DxUi but start new blocks in the reference implementations.
- **Fix**: Compute `const bool paragraphMatched = matched == open_.size() - ...` i.e. whether open\_[matched-1] (the last matched container) is the paragraph's direct parent; pass `paragraphMatched` instead of `true` to HtmlStart and drop the `!marker.empty`/start==1 restriction when the paragraph's own container was not matched (matching cmark's `interrupts_paragraph`). Add directed tests for `> a\n2. b`, `> a\n-`, `> a\n<custom>` against dingus output, and document the chosen interpretation if the literal spec reading is preferred.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: `> a\n2. b` -> cmark and commonmark.js: \<blockquote>\<p>a\</p>\</blockquote>\<ol start="2">\<li>b\</li>\</ol>; DxUi: one paragraph `a\n2. b` inside the quote (Line() reaches src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:1014 with interrupts == false because matched == 1 and start != 1). `> a\n-` (empty item) likewise: references give \<blockquote>\<p>a\</p>\</blockquote>\<ul>\<li>\</li>\</ul>, DxUi gives paragraph `a\n-` in the quote. The same applies at any depth where the last matched container is not a List, e.g. [...]

### blocks-05

**A multi-line paragraph followed by a delimiter row never becomes a table (GFM reference splits the paragraph and uses its last line as header)**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:477` · bug · minor · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: StartTable() rejects any paragraph whose line count is not exactly one. cmark-gfm's row\_from\_string records `paragraph_offset` when the row before the delimiter is preceded by other lines and try\_opening\_table\_header then calls try\_inserting\_table\_header\_paragraph, keeping the earlier lines as a paragraph and converting only the last line into the header (this is how github.com renders `para\n| a | b |\n|---|---|`). The GFM 0.29 spec text does not pin either behaviour and the pinned corpus has no multi-line case, so the conformance test cannot detect the difference.
- **Failure**: `intro\n| a | b |\n| - | - |\n| 1 | 2 |\n` under Gfm029: DxUi produces a single four-line paragraph (lines.size()==2 at the delimiter row), github.com/cmark-gfm produce \<p>intro\</p> followed by a two-column table.
- **Fix**: Use `lines.back()` as the header candidate; when lines.size() > 1 and the header/delimiter column counts match, split the staging paragraph (keep lines[0..n-2] as the Paragraph, create the Table from the last line's range) the same way References() splits definitions from the remainder. Add a directed Gfm029 test and note the behaviour in docs/rich-text.md.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Source `intro\n| a | b |\n| - | - |\n| 1 | 2 |\n` parsed with MarkdownProfile::Gfm029 (default extensions include Tables; the same applies to RichMarkdown). Line 3 reaches StartTable (DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:1100) with the leaf paragraph holding two `lines` entries (one per Content() call, line 547); References() at 468 does nothing because line 1 does not start with '['; the function returns false at 477 because lines.size()==2. [...]

`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:391` · bug · minor · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: HtmlStart returns type 4 for `<!` followed by any ASCII letter via `Letter(text[2])`. The recognizer already distinguishes profiles for `search` (419) and `textarea` (400-401, 429) but not here.

Under CommonMark0312 the type-4 result is correct. [...]

### blocks-07

**acceptedEnd\_/Extend() is a redundant deferred boundary: it always equals lineFirst\_ at every PopContainer and source\_.size() in Finish**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:557` · simplification · minor · confirmed (: confirmed) · plan: W2.13

- **What**: Extend() stores lineEnd\_ in acceptedEnd\_ and is called on all 18 return paths of Line() (47, 616, 633, 1017, 1051, 1063, 1073, 1084, 1096, 1102, 1175, 1192, 1228, 1235, 1254, 1265, 1272, 1286). PopContainer() is only invoked at 1022, 1131 and 1151, all before the current line calls Extend(), and ScanBlockUnit feeds contiguous lines (lineFirst == previous end), so acceptedEnd\_ == lineFirst\_ at every pop; Finish() runs after the last line so acceptedEnd\_ == source\_.size(). The 'deferred accepted container end' the docs describe (docs/rich-text.md:817-818) therefore carries no information; the real optimisation is simply setting source.last at pop time. Any future return path that forgets Extend() would silently truncate container ranges.
- **Failure**: Before: every branch of Line() must end with Extend(); PopContainer reads acceptedEnd\_. After: PopContainer uses `nodes_[closing].block.source.last = lineFirst_`, Finish uses `source_.size()`, and the acceptedEnd\_ member plus 18 Extend() calls are deleted with identical published ranges (verified by CheckMarkdownPartitions and the container-range tests).
- **Fix**: Remove acceptedEnd\_ and Extend(); use lineFirst\_ in PopContainer and source\_.size() in Finish; update the docs sentence about the deferred accepted end to describe pop-time finalisation.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Before: Line() must end every one of its 18 exits with Extend(); PopContainer (572, 575) and Finish (1304, 1306) read the mirrored acceptedEnd\_. After: delete acceptedEnd\_ (line 25), Extend() (557-562) and all 18 Extend() calls; in PopContainer assign `nodes_[closing].block.source.last = lineFirst_;` (and content.last for FootnoteDefinition); in Finish assign `source_.size()` for both; [...]

### blocks-08

**MarkdownBlocks::Line() is a 360-line function mixing five phases and re-running the same lookahead helpers**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownBlocks.h:929` · architecture · minor · confirmed (: confirmed) · plan: W2.13

- **What**: Line() (929-1287) handles source-region envelope, front matter, thematic prepass, container matching, lazy-continuation decision, open-leaf continuation (fence/HTML/indented), blank lines, table close/continue, the container-opening loop, setext, and leaf starts in one body. The interrupt predicate is evaluated twice with different helper sets (1011-1013 vs 1090-1091) and the ATX/fence recognisers exist twice: `Atx()`/`Fence()` for lookahead (269-297) and hand-inlined versions for the actual start (1206, 1238). HtmlStart/ListMarker/Thematic are recomputed per phase on the same cursor.
- **Failure**: Before: one function with ~15 return paths, two divergent interrupt lists and duplicated ATX/fence rules (a future rule change must be applied in two places). After: `MatchContainers(cursor) -> matched`, `ContinueOpenLeaf(cursor, empty) -> bool`, `OpenContainers(cursor)`, `StartLeaf(cursor)` plus one `Interrupts(cursor, probeFootnote)` predicate reused by both call sites and by the start code, with Atx()/Fence() returning the run length consumed by the start path.
- **Fix**: Split Line() into the phases above and make Atx()/Fence()/Thematic() return the data the start path needs so each grammar rule lives in one place.

### blocks-10

**Block scanning spends one Advance work unit per UTF-16 unit just to find line ends**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownSyntax.cpp:405` · performance · minor · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W2.13

- **What**: ScanBlockUnit reads `source[cursor++]` and returns unless the character is CR/LF, so a 65,536-unit document costs >= 65,536 block work units before the per-line Line() call (itself already defined as one bounded indivisible operation). ScanLexicalUnit similarly steps one code point per unit. With the editor's default Advance() budget of 4,096 (DxUi.RichTextEditor.cpp:882) a full-size document needs at least 16 preparation turns for line discovery alone plus 16 for the lexical pass, while RootUnit (427-430) already walks a whole line per unit, so the accounting is inconsistent between phases.
- **Failure**: Before: 64K-unit document with 1,000 lines -> ~66K block units (~17 turns). After: `cursor = source.find_first_of(L"\r\n", cursor)` per unit -> ~1,001 block units (\<1 turn), same published result, and the test guard `work < (MaximumUnits + MaximumNodeRecords) * 32` still holds.
- **Fix**: Make one block work unit equal one physical line (find\_first\_of for the EOL, then Line()), and one lexical unit equal one literal run (find\_first\_of(L"&\\\\\\r\\n\\0")), keeping the documented 'steps not characters' semantics but with far fewer idle steps.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Before: a 65,536-unit RichMarkdown document with ~1,000 lines is set on a RichTextEditor with analysis enabled. PrepareContent calls Advance() with the default 4,096 budget once per owner turn (DxUi.RichTextEditor.cpp:882). ScanBlockUnit charges one work unit per UTF-16 unit (DxUi.MarkdownSyntax.cpp:405-413), so the block phase alone needs ~65,536 units = 16 turns before the roots phase; [...]

## Markdown inline parser (11)

### inlines-01

**Extended-autolink pass resolves a whole text region in one work unit with O(n^2) adversarial scans**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:1167` · performance · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: Step() hands a Text visit to ResolveAutolinks (line 1528-1529), which loops `while (cursor_ < last)` over the entire contiguous Text region of the leaf in a single Advance work unit. Inside, ExtendedAutolink scans the candidate host over the whole remaining region (`while (hostEnd < rest.size()) { ... Contains(kAlphanumeric, scalar) || '_' || '-' || '.' ...}`, lines 1000-1006) before the domain is validated (1012-1014), and the email candidate scans `[alnum . - _ + @]` to the region end (1049-1051) before Email() rejects at the second '@'. A failed candidate advances one code point (1195) and the boundary flags re-arm after '\_' (1197) or after '@' (1198-1199), so the full-length scan repeats at every such position. Nothing in the loop yields; Advance(maximumWorkUnits) cannot interrupt it.
- **Failure**: Paste a 65,536-unit paragraph consisting of `_www.` repeated (or `a@a@a@...`). Each of the ~13,000 (resp. ~32,000) boundary positions rescans to the region end: roughly 0.4-2 billion character visits, each with Scalar() plus a binary search over the ~780-range kAlphanumeric table, inside ONE MarkdownParser::Advance work unit on the owner thread. The editor's preparation turn (RichTextEditor.cpp:882 `state.markdown.Advance()`) freezes the UI for seconds; the step budget provides no relief because the entire region is one step.
- **Fix**: Make the region loop resumable: keep `cursor_`, `current`, `last` and the boundary flags as member state and return from ResolveAutolinks after each candidate (or after N code points) so each Step does bounded work. Bound the candidate scans: reject the www/http host incrementally (stop at the first '\_' in a label, cap host length, stop at the second '.' only when needed) and stop the email scan at the second '@' / first invalid domain character, mirroring cmark-gfm's post-CVE-2023-22486 mitigations. [...]
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Paste a 65,536-unit paragraph of `a@` repeated (32,768 times) into a RichTextEditor with a Gfm029/RichMarkdown profile (extensions default to All, so Autolinks is on). The block/lexical stages produce one paragraph with a single merged Text inline node (Append merges contiguous text, lines 227-235, 1358-1359). On the autolink stage, Step() hands that one Text visit to ResolveAutolinks, which loops over the entire 65,536-unit region in one work unit (1167). [...]

### inlines-02

**AutolinkSplice re-decodes and double-copies the entire remaining node after every spliced link**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:1144` · performance · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: After a link is spliced out of a Text node, the trailing remainder is rebuilt with `current = Append(Kind::Text, last, end, Decode(std::wstring_view(text_).substr(last, end - last)))`. Decode allocates and re-decodes every unit from the link end to the node end, and Append then copies that string again into `node.value` (line 244). Because the remainder becomes the next `current`, the next link in the same region decodes the (slightly shorter) remainder again, so a region with k links costs the sum of all remainders, i.e. O(n\*k), all inside the same single work unit described in inlines-01. The prefix path (line 1129) has the same double copy but is bounded by the inter-link gap.
- **Failure**: A 65,536-unit paragraph of 8,192 `www.a.b ` tokens (a perfectly ordinary URL list) performs ~268 million wchar\_t decode-and-copy operations twice and ~8,192 transient heap allocations of up to 128 KiB each, in one Advance work unit. A 3,000-link paragraph with 20-unit URLs still copies ~100 MB. This is owner-thread preparation time proportional to n^2 for valid input, not an adversarial case.
- **Fix**: Do not materialize the remainder per link. Keep the original node's already-decoded value and a text\_-offset to value-offset table built once per region (or walk value and text\_ in lockstep once), then create suffix nodes by slicing that value with string\_view; move the resulting std::wstring into the node instead of copying through a view. Alternatively create the remainder node lazily only when the loop finishes or when the next link is found, so each unit is decoded at most once per region.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: RichMarkdown/Gfm029 (Autolinks on by default, docs/rich-text.md:874-875) paragraph of 8,192 `www.a.b ` tokens (65,536 units, admitted by MarkdownSyntax.cpp:39-40). Scan produces one merged Text node (DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:1359 + 230-235). Step() calls ResolveAutolinks once for that node (1528-1529); the loop finds all 8,192 links. [...]

### inlines-03

**Rule-of-3 and opener floors use consumed delimiter lengths instead of original run lengths**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:468` · bug · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: Emphasis() decrements the delimiter record's `length` after a match (`open.length -= use; delimiters_[closer].length -= use;`, lines 505-506) and then reuses that same field both for the closer's floor category (`(close.open ? 3u : 0u) + close.length % 3u`, line 458) and for the rule-of-3 test (`(close.length + open.length) % 3u == 0u && (close.length % 3u != 0u || open.length % 3u != 0u)`, lines 468-469). CommonMark 0.31.2 section 6.2 rule 9/10 phrases the rule in terms of 'the lengths of the delimiter runs containing the opening and closing delimiters', i.e. the original runs; commonmark.js keeps `origdelims` separate from `numdelims` and cmark keeps `delimiter->length` untouched while shrinking only the text node. [...]
- **Failure**: Input `**a **b***c` (D1 `**` open-only, D2 `**` open-only, D3 `***` both). D3 matches D2 with use=2 -> Strong(b); D3's length becomes 1 (original 3). Re-evaluating D3 against D1: this code computes (1+2)%3==0 with 1%3!=0 -> 'odd' -> no match, publishing Text(`**a `), Strong(b), Text(`*c`), i.e. `**a <strong>b</strong>*c`. The reference computes (3+2)%3==2 -> match with use=1, giving `*<em>a <strong>b</strong></em>c`. Rendering, structural commands and UIA therefore see a different tree than every conforming renderer for this source.
- **Fix**: Add `std::size_t originalLength` to `Delimiter` (set at line 1350) and use it for `category` and the `odd` test; keep the mutable `length` only for `use` (line 484) and the zero-length removal checks. Add directed tests for partial-match re-evaluation such as `**a **b***c` and `*a **b***c` with expected HTML taken from the reference implementation.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Input '\*\*a \*\*b\*\*\*c' (CommonMark or any profile). Tokenizer (lines 1335-1356): D1 '\*\*' len 2 open-only (White(0) at line 118), D2 '\*\*' len 2 open-only, D3 '\*\*\*' len 3 open+close. Emphasis(MarkdownNoNode): D3 vs D2 -> (3+2)%3=2 not odd -> use=2 -> Strong(b); D2 removed; D3.length=1 (lines 505-506). Loop re-enters with same closer (line 516 non-zero), copies D3 with length 1 (line 451), category 4 (line 458), D1: odd=(1+2)%3==0 && 1%3!=0 -> true (lines 468-469) -> no opener -> D3 kept as literal '\* [...]

### inlines-04

**Emphasis wrappers and autolink child nodes bypass the 32,768-record staging budget**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:488` · bug · major · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: Append (line 236) and OpaqueHtmlBlock (line 296) throw length\_error when `nodes_.size() >= MaximumNodeRecords - blocks_.size()`, but three other node creations do not check: the Emphasis/Strong/Strikethrough wrapper (`nodes_.emplace_back();` line 488), the AngleAutolink child (line 856) and the ExtendedAutolink child (line 1068). MarkdownSyntax.h:237-240 documents MaximumNodeRecords as the 'combined block/inline staging budget' where 'temporary shells count until their compacting pass finishes', docs/rich-text.md:75 repeats the 32,768 bound, and AGENTS.md requires every admission limit to be enforced before allocation. The published count stays under the cap only because the 65,536-unit source cap limits how many wrappers can exist, not because the limit is enforced.
- **Failure**: A 32,700-unit paragraph of `*a*` repeated 10,900 times stages 32,700 nodes through Append (passes: cap is 32,766 with two block nodes). Emphasis(MarkdownNoNode) then appends 10,900 Strong/Emphasis wrappers without any check, so `nodes_` peaks at 43,600 records (33% over the documented staging budget) before Compact() shrinks it. Scope.Tests.MarkdownCapacity.h:44 only asserts the post-compaction count, so the breach is invisible to the suite; the `dense` fixture (12,000 x `*x* `) trips the Append check first and never exercises the wrapper path.
- **Fix**: Route every inline node creation through one checked `Allocate(Kind)` helper that performs the MaximumNodeRecords test and the paired `previous_.push_back`, and use it in Append, OpaqueHtmlBlock, Emphasis, AngleAutolink and ExtendedAutolink. Add a capacity fixture that stages just under the cap and then forces wrappers/children (e.g. `*a*` x 10,900 and `<a@b.c>` lists) and asserts either a length\_error or that peak `nodes_.size()` never exceeds the budget (expose the peak through BlockStatistics-style counters if needed).
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Two concrete inputs, both one paragraph under GFM/RichMarkdown within the 65,536-unit source cap. (A) `*a*` repeated 10,922 times (32,766 units): Scan Appends exactly 32,766 inline records (the Append ceiling with the document+paragraph blocks), then Emphasis(MarkdownNoNode) allocates 10,922 Emphasis wrappers at DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:488 with no budget check, so nodes\_/previous\_ peak at 43,688 staging records, 33% over the documented 32,768 combined staging budget; [...]

**Scan() is 157 lines; Step() and Emphasis() duplicate leaf-finish and cleanup blocks; Lookup runs twice for shortcut refs**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:1203` · simplification · minor · confirmed (: confirmed) · plan: W2.13

- **What**: Scan() (1203-1360) sequences math delimiters, code spans, angle autolinks, raw HTML, backslash escapes, entities, line breaks, bracket push/close and delimiter runs in one function with deeply nested conditions. Step() (1475-1593) contains the same leaf-finish sequence twice (`Emphasis(MarkdownNoNode); blocks_[blockCursor_].firstInline = root_; ++blockCursor_; leafActive_ = ...` at 1542-1545 and 1564-1567). Emphasis() ends with two structurally identical delimiter-cleanup loops that only differ in iteration direction (524-538). CloseBracket calls Lookup on the same bracket text twice when a collapsed `[]` lookup fails (757 then 763-764), folding the label and binary-searching again.
- **Failure**: Before: a reviewer tracing a math/currency or bracket bug must read the whole 157-line Scan and two copies of the finish logic to be sure both paths stay in sync (the autolink and non-autolink finish already differ only by `autolinksActive_`). After: `ScanMath()`, `ScanCode()`, `ScanBreak()`, `ScanDelimiterRun()` helpers called from a short dispatcher, one `FinishLeaf()` used by both Step branches, one forward cleanup loop in Emphasis (the backward variant for bottom==NoNode is equivalent since every live delimiter is removed), and Lookup computed once for the shortcut label.
- **Fix**: Extract per-construct helpers from Scan, a FinishLeaf() helper for Step, collapse the Emphasis cleanup to one loop, and compute the shortcut label once (only re-run Lookup for an explicit different label).
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Better-scoped proposal: (a) Extract only the three heavy blocks from Scan() into private helpers that return bool when they consumed input: ScanMath() (1207-1248, keeps the MathMaximumSourceUnits fallback intact), ScanLineBreak() (1304-1324, trailing-space trim + Soft/HardBreak) and ScanDelimiterRun() (1335-1357, left/right-flanking computation and linked-list push); [...]

### inlines-08

**StartLeaf allocates a fresh backtick index scratch vector for every leaf**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:584` · performance · minor · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W2.13

- **What**: StartLeaf builds the backtick-run index with a local `std::vector<std::size_t> last(text_.size() + 1u, MarkdownNoNode);`, a heap allocation of 8\*(n+1) bytes performed once per inline leaf (every paragraph, heading and table cell, including generated padding cells) and freed at the end of the step. The class already keeps `backtickNext_`, `offsets_`, `previous_` and others as reusable members precisely to avoid per-leaf churn; `last` is the exception.
- **Failure**: A 10,920-row single-column GFM table (the existing capacity fixture) or a document with thousands of short paragraphs performs one allocation and free per leaf during preparation turns on the owner thread, contrary to the 'hot-path resources are bounded and reused' rule; under MSVC Debug the vector also allocates iterator proxies.
- **Fix**: Promote `last` to a member scratch vector (e.g. `backtickLast_`) and `assign()` it per leaf like backtickNext\_, or compute the run index with a single member array indexed by run length capped at text\_.size().
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Every inline leaf (paragraph, ATX/Setext heading, table cell, including generated padding cells and empty cells) constructs and destroys a sized std::vector\<std::size\_t> of text\_.size()+1 elements inside StartLeaf during owner-thread preparation turns. For the 10,920-row single-column GFM table fixture that is 10,920+ malloc/free pairs (and twice that in MSVC Debug due to container proxies) that the neighbouring `backtickNext_.assign()` pattern already avoids. [...]

### inlines-09

**AngleAutolink searches for '>' to the end of the leaf regardless of whitespace**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:835` · performance · minor · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: For every '\<' Scan calls AngleAutolink, which does `const auto end = text_.find(L'>', cursor_ + 1u);` over the whole remaining leaf before checking the content. Absolute-URI and email autolinks cannot contain whitespace, '\<' or control characters (the later loop at 846-848 rejects them), so the search could stop at the first such unit. Each '\<' is its own Scan step, so the cost is per step rather than per region, but an Advance(4096) turn can still visit 4096 x leaf-length units.
- **Failure**: A 65,536-unit paragraph of `<a ` tokens with no '>' makes each of the ~21,000 Scan steps scan ~65k units; one default Advance(4096) turn costs ~268M unit visits (~0.1-0.3 s) and the leaf needs six such turns. The contract hedges 'construct lookahead' as indivisible, so this is a documented limitation rather than a contract breach, but the scan is needlessly unbounded.
- **Fix**: Scan forward unit by unit and stop at the first whitespace/control/'\<' (or at a 1-2k unit cap) before concluding no autolink; HtmlEnd keeps its own grammar-driven scan.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: A single-line paragraph of 65,536 '\<' units (RichMarkdown or GFM profile, source cap exactly reached). Block scanning accepts it as one paragraph; StartLeaf builds text\_ of 65,536 units with no '>' index. Every Step() calls Scan() on a '\<': AngleAutolink's text\_.find(L'>', cursor\_+1) scans the entire remaining tail (average ~32k units) and returns npos, HtmlEnd returns 0 at line 910, and lines 1358-1359 append one unit of Text. [...]

**\\(...\\) inherits the single-dollar nonspace-edge and trailing-digit rules but docs state them only for \$...\$**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:1231` · doc-mismatch · minor · confirmed (: confirmed) · plan: W1.6

- **What**: Scan treats `\(` as a width-2, non-display opener (line 1212: `display = width == 2u && (dollars || text_[first + 1u] == L'[')`), so the content-edge test `(display || ! SourceSpace(text_[content]))` (1215) and the closer test `(! display && (SourceSpace(text_[end - 1u]) || (end + width < text_.size() && Digit(text_[end + width]))))` (1231) apply to `\(...\)` exactly as to `$...$`. docs/rich-text.md:1217-1219 says 'Single-dollar math is single-line and requires nonspace content edges; a closing dollar followed by a digit is currency text' and lists `\(...\)` without those restrictions; LaTeX users routinely write `\( x \)`.
- **Failure**: RichMarkdown source `\( x^2 \)` or `\(x\)5` is published as literal Text rather than a Math inline, with no diagnostic, while the documentation implies both should be recognized; the currency heuristic has no meaning for the backslash form.

### inlines-11

**Escaped table-cell pipe leaves a one-unit source hole and splits otherwise contiguous Text nodes**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownInlines.h:560` · bug · minor · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W1.3

- **What**: In TableCell leaves StartLeaf skips the backslash of `\|` (`if (... source_[i] == L'\\' && ... source_[i + 1u] == L'|') ++i; offsets_.push_back(i);`, 560-562), so no `offsets_` entry refers to the backslash. Range() (218-226) derives `source.first` from `offsets_[first]`, so a Text node that begins at the escaped pipe starts at the '\|' and the preceding node ends at the backslash: the backslash belongs to no inline, contradicting MarkdownSyntax.h:124-126 ('source encloses delimiters and any stripped line prefixes'). Append's merge test `nodes_[tail_].source.last == range.first` (230) then fails across the hole, so `a\|b` becomes two Text nodes `a` {0,1} and `|b` {2,4}. [...]
- **Failure**: GFM table cell `a\|b`: published Inlines() for the cell are Text('a') source {c,c+1} and Text('\|b') source {c+2,c+4}; unit c+1 (the backslash) is in no inline and the value is needlessly fragmented into two nodes (and two semantic runs). Tools that assert inline ranges tile the leaf content, or that count nodes against the 32,768 budget, are off by one per escaped pipe.
- **Fix**: Record the backslash as the source start of the '\|' unit (e.g. push `i - 1` for the pipe and let Range's end computation add 2 for an escaped pipe, mirroring the CRLF special case), or keep a per-unit width table so Range covers `\|` as one two-unit source span; then the merge test succeeds and the forest tiles the cell.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: GFM/RichMarkdown table cell whose content is `a\|b` at source offset c: Published Inlines() for that leaf are Text("a") with source {c,c+1} and Text("\|b") with source {c+2,c+4}. Unit c+1 (the backslash) belongs to no inline node, and the literal value is split into two Text nodes where every other escape (e.g. `a\*b`) yields one merged Text node whose source encloses the backslash. [...]

## Document core and v0 layout (18)

### document-01

**Draw contains an unreachable lazy whole-layout preparation path that would do layout/heap work during raster drawing**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichDocumentLayout.cpp:642` · architecture · minor · confirmed (: confirmed) · plan: W2.1

- **What**: PreparedDocumentLayout::Draw (lines 639-655) computes referenceRaster = referenceForGeometry \|\| reference and, inside that branch, has `if (! state.reference) { JoinBlocks(...); reference->PrepareOwned(state.factory.get(), joined, state.options); state.reference = ...; ++state.last.wholeLayoutsPrepared; }`. That branch requires referenceForGeometry == true with reference == null, which cannot happen: the Reference phase (lines 405-440) sets referenceForGeometry only under `! request.exactPlacement`, and in that case it always either reuses state\_->reference or creates one (`else if (! request.exactPlacement)` at 422-430) before reading `next.reference->Height()` at 435. [...]
- **Failure**: Today: unreachable, so no runtime effect, but const-correctness of the published State is eroded (two mutable members) and a maintainer adding a path that publishes referenceForGeometry without a reference (e.g., dropping the reference on an options change to save memory) would silently turn every paint into an O(document) JoinBlocks + CreateTextLayout.
- **Fix**: Delete the lazy branch in Draw (keep `state.reference->Draw` under `referenceRaster`), drop `mutable` from State::reference and State::last, and add an assertion/invariant comment at Publish that referenceForGeometry implies reference. Consider keeping the factory only in Preparation (needed across Advance turns) and storing a non-owning identity token in the published State if the ABA concern is addressed otherwise.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Dead code with eroded const-correctness, not a live defect. Today every published State satisfies referenceForGeometry => reference != nullptr, so Draw never joins blocks or creates a DirectWrite layout. The latent hazard, if a future change published a State with referenceForGeometry set and reference null (e.g., dropping the whole-layout reference on an options change), is that every RichTextEditor::Paint would run an O(document) JoinBlocks plus IDWriteFactory::CreateTextLayout through a const [...]

### document-02

**BeginPreparation clones the whole published projection and then Prepare rebuilds a second full block index from the clone**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichDocumentLayout.cpp:250` · performance · minor · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W2.13

- **What**: BeginPreparation does `next.projection = state_->projection.Clone();` (line 250) then `next.projection.Prepare(source, revision, mode);` (line 253). PreparedProjection::Clone (RichMarkdownProjection.cpp:234-240) copies the entire State including the `std::vector<ProjectionBlock> blocks` (one shared\_ptr copy per physical line). Prepare (lines 253-336) then allocates a fresh State and copies the reused prefix/suffix blocks from the clone again (lines 287 and 318) before `state_.swap(next)`, discarding the clone's vector. For an edit in the middle of a document this is two O(lines) vector allocations and 2x(lines) atomic shared\_ptr increments/decrements per keystroke, where one would do. [...]
- **Failure**: A 65,536-unit source consisting of 32,768 CRLF-terminated lines: each keystroke copies a 32,768-entry vector (512 KiB) twice and performs ~131,000 atomic refcount operations before any DirectWrite work starts, all inside the indivisible Begin step that the host cannot budget.
- **Fix**: Add `PreparedProjection::PrepareFrom(const PreparedProjection& base, SourceSnapshot, revision, mode)` (or make Prepare take an optional base) that builds the new State directly from the published base's blocks without an intermediate clone; keep Clone for tests/other callers. Reserve `next->blocks` to `first + estimated + (size - last)` to avoid regrowth.

### document-03

**Every recorded edit, undo and redo copies the entire 128-entry history vector for exception safety**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichDocument.cpp:725` · performance · minor · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W2.13

- **What**: ReplaceImpl does `nextUndo.emplace(undo_)` (line 725), i.e., a full copy of up to MaximumHistory (128) History entries (each a SourceSnapshot shared\_ptr + Selection) followed by Push and `undo_.swap(*nextUndo)` (731). Undo (747) and Redo (761) likewise copy `redo_`/`undo_` wholesale. The copy exists only so that Push's potential push\_back allocation cannot leave the document half-updated. Because History copy/move is noexcept (shared\_ptr) and Push evicts before appending (596-603), reserving MaximumHistory once (in Reset) makes `erase(begin()) + push_back` non-throwing, which removes a ~3 KiB allocation plus 128 atomic increments and 128 decrements from every keystroke, Undo and Redo.
- **Failure**: Typing 1,000 characters into a document with a full 128-entry undo stack performs 1,000 vector allocations of 128 x 24 bytes and 256,000 atomic refcount operations that do no useful work; on MSVC Debug each vector copy also allocates an iterator proxy, which the code elsewhere goes to lengths to avoid.
- **Fix**: Call `undo_.reserve(MaximumHistory); redo_.reserve(MaximumHistory);` in Reset (the only place that clears them), make Push noexcept relying on reserved capacity (assert `history.capacity() >= MaximumHistory`), and mutate undo\_/redo\_ in place after the snapshot/Revision allocation has succeeded.
- **Verifier (, confirmed)**: Typing N characters into a RichTextEditor after the first 128 committed edits: each OnChar -> Document::ReplaceSelection -> ReplaceImpl(recordHistory=true) performs nextUndo.emplace(undo\_) (copy of 128 x 32-byte History entries = one 4 KiB allocation plus 128 interlocked shared\_ptr increments), Push on the copy (noexcept 127-element shift + push\_back), swap, and then destruction of the displaced old vector at scope exit (128 interlocked decrements, one free); [...]

**VisibleRange issues one DirectWrite HitTestTextPosition per line (including a phantom terminal entry) although line heights are already retained**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextLayout.cpp:468` · performance · minor · confirmed (: confirmed; : confirmed) · plan: W2.13

- **What**: PreparedLayout::VisibleRange loops over every entry of `state_->lines` and calls `Caret(state_->lines[index])` (line 468), i.e., `IDWriteTextLayout::HitTestTextPosition` per line, to learn each line's top/height. State already stores `lineMetrics` (line 73) with per-line heights, so line tops are `verticalOrigin + prefix-sum(height)` with no SDK call. `lines` is built as `{0}` plus prefix sums of every line length (314-316) so its last entry always equals text.size(); when the text ends with '\\n' DirectWrite reports an extra empty line, producing a duplicate entry (e.g. [0,3,3]) that is hit-tested twice. [...]
- **Failure**: A document that fell back to whole geometry (UsesWholeGeometry) with 4,000 wrapped lines answers each GetVisibleSourceRange with up to 4,001 HitTestTextPosition calls even when the viewport shows 30 lines, because the loop only breaks once `caret.y >= bottom` and starts from line 0 every time.
- **Fix**: Store cumulative line tops (float prefix sums via AddCoordinate, consistent with continuationBottom) next to `lines`, binary-search the first line whose bottom > top and the last whose top \< bottom, and return their display offsets; drop the duplicate terminal entry or treat it explicitly.
