## Completeness critic additions (5)

### critic-01

**Multi-file reference paste inserts bare blank-line separators, breaking the enclosing table, list or block quote**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextEditor.cpp:289` · bug · major · confirmed (refute: confirmed; reproduce: confirmed) · plan: W1.4

- **What**: TryPrepareClipboardPaste joins file references with `staged.append(eol).append(eol)` (285-290) and OnKeyDown 'V' inserts the result with a raw ReplaceSelection at the caret (1890-1891). The separator has no container continuation prefix (`> `, list-item indentation). CanPasteMarkdownFileReferences (190-239) excludes code, HTML, front matter, literal notes and partial inline tokens, but not GFM table cells, list items or block quotes. The spec says 'Multiple files MUST use blank paragraphs' (UI\_ControlsAndLayout.md:806-807) but does not account for containers that cannot hold a blank line. Existing tests (Scope.Tests.RichTextInput.cpp:367-390) only paste into a top-level paragraph.
- **Failure**: Steps: 1. RichMarkdown Rich mode, saved document C:\\docs\\note.md with source `| h1 | h2 |\r\n| -- | -- |\r\n| a | b |\r\n| c | d |\r\n`. 2. Put the caret after 'b' and paste an Explorer list {C:\\docs\\x.png, C:\\docs\\y.png}. The source becomes `| a | b![x] (x.png)\r\n\r\n![y] (y.png) |\r\n| c | d |`. The blank line ends the table, so `![y] (y.png) |` and every later row `| c | d |` render as paragraph text with literal pipes. In `- item` or `> quote`, the second file becomes a top-level paragraph outside the list or quote, and later items start a new list or quote. [...]
- **Fix**: Make the separator container-aware: repeat the caret line's container prefix (quote markers and list content indentation) after each EOL. Inside a table cell, either reject structured multi-file paste and fall back to the quoted literal list, or join references with a single space so they stay in the cell. Add native and embedded tests for table, list and quote contexts.
- **Verifier (refute, confirmed)**: As stated. Rich mode, document path C:\\docs\\note.md, source `| h1 | h2 |\r\n| -- | -- |\r\n| a | b |\r\n| c | d |\r\n`. With the caret after 'b', paste the file list {C:\\docs\\x.png, C:\\docs\\y.png} with Ctrl+V. The source becomes `| a | b![x] (x.png)\r\n\r\n![y] (y.png) |\r\n| c | d |`. GFM ends the table at the blank line, so `![y] (y.png) |` and the following `| c | d |` line form one paragraph with literal pipes. [...]
- **Existing tests**: No test covers a table, list or block-quote context. Tests/Support/Scope.Tests.RichImagePaths.h:139-150 and 300-308 and Tests/Controls/Scope.Tests.RichTextInput.cpp:377-384 cover only top-level or empty documents.

### critic-02

**Paste (and TSF/IME import) at an empty or padded table cell inserts at the shared row-end ACP instead of materializing the focused cell**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextEditor.cpp:1893` · bug · minor · confirmed (refute: confirmed; reproduce: confirmed) · plan: W1.4

- **What**: OnChar routes typing at a cell scaffold through SetTableCell, which escapes `|` and `\` and materialises the focused padded cell (1783-1795, docs/rich-text.md:1077-1078). Ctrl+V has no such branch: it calls ReplaceSelection(content.text) or the file-reference text raw at the caret (1890-1893). For a padded cell the caret ACP is the row's content end before the EOL, shared by every padded cell of that row (RichSemanticGeometry.cpp:689-702: 'M3's padded token is anchored at the full source row end ... Its visual insertion site is before that EOL'). Raw insertion therefore lands after the last real cell, not in the focused column. Pasted `|` is not escaped either. The spec makes text-service imports raw ('Typing and text-service imports retain exact source replacement semantics. [...]
- **Failure**: Steps: 1. Source `| h1 | h2 | h3 |\r\n| -- | -- | -- |\r\n| a |\r\n`. The body row has padded cells 2 and 3. 2. Click the padded cell under h3 and press Ctrl+V with the clipboard holding 'x'. The source becomes `| a |x\r\n`, which GFM parses as cells 'a','x', so 'x' appears under h2, not h3. With the row spelled `| a` (no trailing pipe) the result is `| ax`, merged into column 1. Typing 'x' at the same site correctly produces a third-column cell through SetTableCell. Pasting 'p\|q' into an empty real cell splits it into two cells.
- **Fix**: In the paste path (and in the embedded TransformClipboardPaste), when IsCellSourceSite is true and the pasted text is single-line, build a SetTableCell request with pipes and backslashes escaped, as OnChar does. For multi-line text at a cell, fall back to replacing EOLs with spaces or reject the structured paste. Consider the same materialisation for a TSF commit at a padded site, and amend UI\_ControlsAndLayout.md:1244-1246 accordingly.
- **Verifier (refute, confirmed)**: Native host, Rich semantic mode, source `| h1 | h2 | h3 |\r\n| -- | -- | -- |\r\n| a |\r\n`. Click the padded cell under h3 (the caret sits at the shared row-end ACP before CRLF) and press Ctrl+V with the clipboard holding 'x'. The source becomes `| a |x\r\n`, so 'x' renders under h2. Typing 'x' at the same site materializes column 3 through SetTableCell. With the row spelled `| a`, paste at either padded cell gives `| ax`, merged into column 1. [...]
- **Existing tests**: No test pastes at a padded or empty cell. Scope.Tests.RichSemanticEditor.h:173-175 covers typing ('typing materializes only the focused padded cell and escapes a literal pipe'), and 556-605 covers click/navigation/TSF sync without mutation.

### critic-03

**Ctrl+X of a content-only formatted selection leaves a visible empty delimiter pair that Delete would remove**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichTextEditor.cpp:1864` · bug · minor · confirmed (refute: uncertain; reproduce: confirmed) · plan: W1.4

- **What**: Erase on a non-empty Rich-mode selection runs BuildMarkdownDeletion, whose fixed point expands a wrapper whose entire body is deleted (RichTextCommands.cpp:2006-2023), as the spec requires ('Semantic visual deletion MUST remove empty owned Markdown/validated inline HTML wrappers only when their complete body is deleted', UI\_ControlsAndLayout.md:1336-1337). Cut copies the same interval and then removes it with raw `ReplaceSelection({})`, so the identical user selection gives different source depending on whether the user pressed Delete or Ctrl+X. Native Cut is a keyboard command, not TSF or programmatic replacement, so the raw-replacement exemption does not obviously apply. Tests cover cut only for a whole `**β**` selection (Scope.Tests.RichTextEditor.cpp:268-274).
- **Failure**: Steps: 1. Rich mode, source `x **bold** y`. 2. Double-click 'bold'. SelectSingleLineWordAt plus SourceDisplayMap::ToSource gives the source selection [4,8), the content only, because hidden delimiters are not display spans (RichSemantic.cpp:58-60, 149-171). Delete yields `x y`: the wrapper [2,10) is expanded and removed. Ctrl+X yields `x **** y`, which renders literally as 'x \*\*\*\* y'. The asterisks become visible text and the user must delete them manually.
- **Fix**: After a successful copy, have native Cut in Rich mode call the same Erase selection path (BuildMarkdownDeletion plus ApplyMarkdownCommand). Decide in the spec whether the embedded TextInputServices cut follows the same rule, then add a native and embedded test on `x **bold** y`.
- **Verifier (refute, uncertain)**: Native host, Rich semantic mode, source `x **bold** y`. Double-click 'bold', which selects source [4,8). Delete gives `x y` because the wrapper [2,10) is removed. Ctrl+X instead copies 'bold' and raw-removes [4,8), leaving `x **** y`, which renders literal asterisks. Embedded Ctrl+X gives the same raw result through TextInputServices, which the contract makes raw. Whether native Cut must use semantic deletion is not specified.
- **Existing tests**: Scope.Tests.RichTextEditor.cpp:261-274 (whole `**β**` selected, not semantic Rich mode) and 861-870 (whole diagram source cut) pin raw/exact cut but not a content-only formatted selection. No test pins either outcome for `x **bold** y`.

### critic-04

**Scoped test rules for src/include Rich\* omit the Accessibility suite that runs the rich UIA provider tests**  
`Tests/test-scopes.json:159` · test-gap · minor · confirmed (feasibility: confirmed) · plan: T3

- **What**: Get-ScopedTestPlan (Tools/ScopedTesting.psm1:96-102) unions the scopes of every matching rule and uses the full fallback only for unmatched paths. The rule `src/Controls/DxUi.Rich*` (scopes EditorControls, Embedded) matches DxUi.RichAccessibility.h (the RichSemanticElementProvider and UIA rich children), DxUi.RichAccessibilityEpoch.h and DxUi.RichSemanticDocument.cpp, which produces the UIA Name/HelpText strings. `include/DxUi/Rich*` (line 151) is the same. However, Scope.Tests.RichTextAccessibility.cpp runs only from the Accessibility suite (DxUi.Tests.Accessibility.cpp:9 includes its header, and :10229 calls RunRichTextAccessibilityTests). [...]
- **Failure**: A developer changes the UIA\_HelpTextPropertyId branch in src/Controls/DxUi.RichAccessibility.h:249-257, or a diagram title in RichSemanticDocument.cpp:698-700, and runs `Test-Changes.ps1` in its default scoped mode. Only EditorControls and Embedded are selected, so every native rich UIA provider assertion in Scope.Tests.RichTextAccessibility.cpp is skipped and the regression is first seen at PrePush/CI, or later.
- **Fix**: Add "Accessibility" to the src/Controls/DxUi.Rich\* and include/DxUi/Rich\* rules, or add a narrower rule `src/Controls/DxUi.RichAccessibility*` → [Accessibility, Embedded]. Add a tooling test asserting that every source file included by DxUi.Accessibility.cpp selects the Accessibility scope.
- **Verifier (feasibility, confirmed)**: A developer edits UIA\_HelpTextPropertyId handling in src/Controls/DxUi.RichAccessibility.h:249-257, or a diagnostic/footnote string in src/Controls/DxUi.RichSemanticDocument.cpp. The default `Test-Changes.ps1` plan matches only the Rich\* rule and selects EditorControls+Embedded (+Tooling). None of the 23 rich Name/HelpText assertions in Scope.Tests.RichTextAccessibility.cpp run, so the regression first appears under PrePush/Full or CI.
- **Existing tests**: None. Test-ScopedTesting.ps1 covers Grid fan-out (:142-144), ComboBox->NewControls (:162-166) and the tooling fixtures, but has no Rich/Accessibility mapping assertion.

### critic-05

**Rich UIA names, help texts and diagnostics are hard-coded English with no consumer override**  
`src/Controls/DxUi.RichSemanticDocument.cpp:640` · design-gap · minor · confirmed (feasibility: confirmed) · plan: W2.14

- **What**: The semantic builder writes English phrases into SemanticElement::name and ::title, and the provider exposes them directly as UIA Name and HelpText (RichAccessibility.h:242-257). Examples: - Footnote names: 'Footnote N', 'Go to footnote N.', 'Return to reference K for footnote N' (406-407, 875, 962). - Diagram summaries: 'Flowchart, ' / 'State diagram, ' plus 'top to bottom' and similar (640-649). - Element titles: 'Flowchart. Source is available as document text.' (698-700), 'Mathematical expression...' (337) and 'Editing exact block source.' (750). - Diagnostics: 'Malformed diagram. [...]
- **Failure**: A French RedSalamander build uses RichTextEditor with a Mermaid flowchart and a footnote. Narrator announces 'Flowchart, left to right, ...' and 'Go to footnote 1.', and the F2 hint and diagnostics are read in English. The application has no API to supply localized text, so the only workaround is to fork library code.
- **Fix**: Add a RichTextEditor::SetAccessibleStrings(RichTextAccessibleStrings) value type with English defaults: whole-phrase templates such as 'Footnote {0}', diagram family and direction names, the F2 hints, and a per-SemanticDiagnosticKind message table. Apply the strings at UIA query time from SemanticDiagnosticKind and the structured fields rather than baking text into SemanticElement. Document the hook in docs/controls.md and docs/rich-text.md.
- **Verifier (feasibility, confirmed)**: A consumer localizing its UI places a RichTextEditor with Markdown analysis and semantic rendering enabled, containing a Mermaid flowchart and a footnote. UIA Name for the diagram starts with 'Flowchart, left to right. Nodes: ...', footnote links read 'Go to footnote 1.' / 'Return to reference 1 for footnote 1', and source leaves expose 'Invoke or press F2 to edit exact source.' as HelpText. Neither RichTextEditor nor SemanticBuilder offers a way to supply translated text. [...]
- **Existing tests**: Scope.Tests.RichTextAccessibility.cpp:1250-1292 asserts the exact English HelpText (e.g. L"Invoke or press F2 to edit exact source.") and metadata diagnostic help. No test covers a consumer-supplied or localized string, because no API exists for one.

## Coverage the critic found missing

- **coverage/files**: Files no partition opened: include/DxUi/Typography.h (538 lines) and src/Controls/DxUi.Typography.h; src/Controls/DxUi.MarkdownEntityData.h (2,143 generated lines, structure only); Tools/Generate-MarkdownEntityData.ps1 and Tools/Generate-MarkdownUnicodeData.ps1 (only their -Check freshness test was seen); include/DxUi/PreparedTreeAccessibility.h; Tests/Support/Scope.Tests.ClipboardFixture.h; Tests/Controls/Controls.Tests.TextStoreComposition.h; [...] Why: Docs cite the Measurements READMEs as qualification evidence and the design-system preview as the UI guideline; AGENTS.md requires both to be truthful. The test-scope manifest decides which suites a rich change runs. Check: Diff each Measurements/RichText README claim (limits, turns, supported families) against RichMath.h, NativeFlowchart.cpp and docs/rich-text.md. Compare preview.html states (F2 for math, link hover, palette) with RichTextEditor.cpp and RichAccessibility.h. [...]
- **editing semantics (selection replacement)**: Nobody cross-checked the policy for replacing a selection in Rich mode across Delete/Backspace, Cut, typing, Ctrl+V and TSF import. Erase (RichTextEditor.cpp:1626-1633) passes the raw selection interval to BuildMarkdownDeletion, as docs/rich-text.md:1202-1203 documents. Cut, typing and paste use raw ReplaceSelection with no wrapper cleanup. So selecting 'ld ta' in `**bold** tail` and pressing Delete gives `**boil`: the hidden closing delimiter is deleted and asterisks appear. [...] Why: Ordinary visual selections can turn hidden Markdown into visible markup. Users see the formatting break and literal asterisks appear, even though no delimiter was visible or selected. Check: Retained-editor tests in both hosts on `**bold** tail`: double-click 'bold' then Delete vs Ctrl+X; drag 'ld ta' then Delete, a typed character, Ctrl+V and TSF commit. Compare the resulting source and display. [...]
- **structured paste in containers**: CanPasteMarkdownFileReferences (RichTextEditor.cpp:190-239) excludes code, HTML, metadata, literal notes and partial inline tokens, but not table cells, list items or block quotes. Multi-file paste joins references with bare `eol eol` (285-290), and every paste bypasses the table-cell materialisation that OnChar performs (1783-1795). See critic-01 and critic-02. Why: A supported, documented feature corrupts table, list or quote structure. In a table every later body row becomes paragraph text. Check: In native and embedded hosts, paste a two-file CF\_HDROP with the caret in a GFM body cell, after `- item` and after `> quote`, and assert the table, list and quote counts in the reparsed MarkdownSyntax. [...]
- **test-scope registration**: Tests/test-scopes.json:159 maps `src/Controls/DxUi.Rich*` to EditorControls and Embedded only, and :151 does the same for `include/DxUi/Rich*`. DxUi.RichAccessibility.h, DxUi.RichAccessibilityEpoch.h, DxUi.RichSemanticDocument.cpp (UIA Name/HelpText) and RichSemanticDocument.h all match, but the native rich UIA tests (Scope.Tests.RichTextAccessibility.cpp, run from DxUi.Tests.Accessibility.cpp:9/10229) are in the Accessibility scope (critic-04). [...] Why: A scoped iteration run after editing the rich UIA provider skips its own provider tests. A UIA regression can then reach PrePush unnoticed. Check: Run Tools/Test-Changes.ps1 -Explain -Paths src/Controls/DxUi.RichAccessibility.h and check whether Accessibility is selected. Fix by adding Accessibility to the src and include Rich\* rules, or by adding a dedicated DxUi.RichAccessibility\* rule.
- **localization**: About 25 hard-coded English strings reach UIA Name/HelpText: RichSemanticDocument.cpp:337, 344, 406-407, 552-637, 640-649, 698-700, 750, 875, 885-886, 962 and RichAccessibility.h:253/255. RichTextEditor has no override hook (critic-05). Why: Consumers such as RedSalamander cannot localize screen-reader output for footnotes, diagrams, math and the F2 hints. The library's documented pattern is an English default plus a consumer override (SetStepButtonNames, ColorPicker SetLabels, ComboBox SetNoMatchesText). Check: grep `L"[A-Z]` in RichSemanticDocument.cpp and RichAccessibility.h and confirm that each string reaches UIA\_NamePropertyId or UIA\_HelpTextPropertyId (RichAccessibility.h:242-257). Check docs/controls.md for a RichTextEditor localization statement.
- **exception containment across PrepareContent**: semantic-01 analysed only the builder stage. Deterministic logic\_error, invalid\_argument and overflow\_error from geometry and diagram preparation also reach PrepareContent's generic catch (RichTextEditor.cpp:1079-1087), which returns E\_FAIL with no failure memo. Throw sites: RichSemanticGeometry.cpp:569, 715, 799, 826, 838, 1004; PreparedFlowchart.cpp:119, 128, 172-180; NativeFlowchartLayout.cpp:250 ('DAG ranking made no progress'), 462-476. Each retry rebuilds the same failure. Why: One document that trips any of these invariants never publishes a layout and consumes owner turns on every host pass. W1.2's memo must cover the geometry and diagram stages, not only the builder. Check: Inject a throw at PreparedSemanticGeometry::Advance and at the PreparedFlowchart label-metrics check, drive the native and embedded hosts for N idle passes, and count PrepareContent/AdvancePreparation calls via DebugGetState. Expect a single attempt followed by source fallback.
- **UIA threading (verified, residual)**: Verified: the snapshot is std::atomic\<std::shared\_ptr\<const AccessibilitySnapshot>> (DxUi.Accessibility.cpp:979). AccessibilityRichTextSnapshot is built on the owner thread (2100-2127). SemanticDocument and SourceDisplayMap have no mutable members. The epoch fetch\_add uses release (RichTextEditor.cpp:453) and the read uses acquire (Accessibility.cpp:571). [...] Why: A single direct editor call from a UIA thread would race the owner thread's preparation and mutation. Check: In DxUi.Accessibility.cpp and RichAccessibility.h, list every `editor->` / `RichTextEditor` use and confirm each sits inside an Execute\*OnWindowThread or owner-dispatch lambda. Optionally stress-test with a UIA client thread calling GetText/Move/GetBoundingRectangles while the owner types and reflow [...]
- **theme / high contrast**: No partition checked the colours of native objects. Paint draws diagram strokes and table rules with caretBrush, which is style.caret = theme.focusStroke (RichTextEditor.cpp:1397, 1416). The diagram fill is style.fill with alpha forced to 1 (1402-1404), so a consumer palette with a translucent inputFill paints that colour's raw RGB opaquely. Selected images are drawn at 0.65 opacity. There is no high-contrast or dark gallery oracle for math or diagrams. Why: Contrast and theming of objects are user-visible accessibility requirements, and the rule colours are not specified in UI\_ThemeAndTypography.md. Check: Generate gallery tiles for math, flowchart and table with the HighContrast palette (Theme.cpp ~998) and with a custom ThemePalette whose inputFill alpha is below 1. Check WCAG contrast of edge and label against fill. Read UI\_ThemeAndTypography.md for which tokens the rich roles should use.
- **bidi / RTL in math and diagrams**: PreparedMath always uses Cambria Math, en-US and LTR (PreparedMath.h:318-325). Flowchart labels inherit the editor's RTL reading direction (PreparedFlowchart.cpp:95), while diagram geometry is never mirrored. The alternative text says 'left to right' and similar. No RTL raster or placement fixture covers inline math, display math or a flowchart in an RTL editor. Why: Neutral characters around inline objects and diagram labels can reorder in RTL paragraphs. The behaviour is unspecified and untested. Check: Add a FlowDirection::RightToLeft fixture containing `$x+1$`, a `$$` block and a two-node flowchart with Latin and Hebrew labels. Compare glyph origins and label alignment in native and embedded hosts, and decide the contract in UI\_ControlsAndLayout.md.
- **high-DPI rounding**: Math rule and fraction-bar thickness is std::max(1.f, font/20) in unscaled math units and is then multiplied by the fit scale (PreparedMath.h:361-365), so it can fall below one device pixel. Rule lines, diagram strokes and image placements are not snapped to pixels, and no math or diagram raster runs at 125, 150 or 175 percent (tests-host-01 only notes the missing DPI coverage). Why: Fraction bars and diagram edges can blur or disappear at fractional DPI and when scaled to fit. Check: Render a scaled-to-fit `\frac{a}{b}` and a flowchart at 120, 144 and 168 DPI in both hosts and measure the bar's ink coverage. Grep for pixel snapping (floor or round with pixelsPerDip) in PreparedMath and PreparedFlowchart.
- **ARM64 floating-point determinism**: RichDocumentLayout.cpp:19 and RichSemanticGeometry.cpp:240 use `volatile float` to stop contraction. That implies the authors saw fused multiply-add sensitivity, yet the accumulations in NativeFlowchartLayout.cpp, PreparedMath Measure/Place and the rest of the geometry code have no such guard. Why: Pixel oracles that are byte-identical on x64 may differ on native ARM64. That runtime work is known and pending, but this specific risk is not recorded anywhere. Check: Check /fp settings in src/DxUi.vcxproj for ARM64. Build ARM64 Release with /FAs and grep the assembly for fmadd/fmsub in layout functions, or add /fp:contract- and compare oracles when native ARM64 runs.
- **undo/redo with native objects and F2**: No partition tested undo or redo across an F2 envelope (UpdateSemanticSourceRegion, RichTextEditor.cpp:576-596, silently retires the envelope on an outside change), undo restoring a caret inside an atomic math, diagram or image source, or image request churn after undoing unrelated text (Changed() calls images.Cancel on every source change). Why: These are user-visible interaction contracts for every extension object, and W1.1 and W3.4 only cover parts of them. Check: Retained-editor tests in both hosts: (1) F2 into a code block, Ctrl+Z an earlier outside edit, and assert the envelope exits and accessibility retires; (2) undo to a selection inside `$x$` and assert caret snapping and Delete; [...]
- **security of pasted and authored destinations**: Beyond images-clipboard-01 (`//host`), these spellings also resolve to UNC requests by design: `\\server\share\x.png` written with Markdown-escaped backslashes, `file://server/share/x.png`, and WebDAV server names such as `//server@SSL/...` (no `@` check in the non-file branch, RichImagePaths.h:255-261). The contract leaves UNC to the caller (UI\_ControlsAndLayout.md:849), but the planned fix for images-clipboard-01 (reject a leading `//`) would not cover the other spellings. Why: A consumer that enables network files gets SMB or WebDAV requests driven by the document. The policy should be stated once and tested for every spelling. Check: Add Scope.Tests.RichImagePaths vectors for each spelling. Read Samples/EmbeddedControls/RichImageLoader.h for the allowNetworkFiles default and document the recommended consumer policy in docs/rich-text.md.
- **paint-time work**: Paint is const but calls RequireCurrentLayout, which runs UpdateCaretScroll and mutates scroll state. It also allocates on every frame: the SourceRange vector and an underline vector per composition clause. editor-05 and editor-06 cover the cost but not the const-mutation contract. Embedded rasterization counts as preparation, so this is not a composition violation. Why: Paint's purity, and the absence of hidden state changes during a raster pass, are relied on by the hidden/visible retirement logic. Check: Count allocations in RichTextEditor::Paint with the existing thread\_local operator-new counters across a caret-blink repaint, both with and without a selection. Assert that scrollDip does not change during Paint.
- **copy of rendered objects**: Copy always transfers the exact source interval (documented). There is no rendered or plain-text alternative (math alternative text, diagram description), and copying a selection that crosses a hidden delimiter yields unbalanced Markdown when pasted elsewhere. Why: This is a user-facing clipboard design decision that no partition owned. It is not in the gap inventory except as 'paste-as-plain-text'. Check: Owner decision. Add a retained-editor test that copies 'ld ta' from `**bold** tail` and pastes it into a new editor, and record the expected behaviour in docs/rich-text.md.
- **claims never verified**: Still unverified: - 143 findings with no verifier vote (accessibility, geometry, host-integration, images-clipboard, math, mermaid, semantic, part of commands and tests-host). The third pass is pending. - The judge's analytic estimate of about 300 owner turns for a 1,000-paragraph document. - host-integration-03's figure of about 30 whole-document copies per WM\_MOUSEMOVE. - The Mermaid fan-out size estimates. - The gap-inventory coverage numbers: 13 of 105 flowchart examples, 8 of 20 state fence [...] Why: The plan sequences waves and the D4c decision on these numbers. Unpaired or analytic numbers do not establish anything under AGENTS.md. Check: Finish verify3. Add a corpus harness that runs ParseFlowchart and ParseMath over every pinned Mermaid 11.4.1 fence and every KaTeX support-table row and prints the admitted counts. Measure turns-to-ready with DebugGetState on the W0.1 fixture.
